Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The galaxies variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 558 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 877 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 558 + O(1/n^877). Summing over all modes and applying the galaxies convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
